- [ ] **Step 3: 写材料页的失败用例**

`case-views/material-facts.test.tsx`：

```tsx
import { afterEach, describe, expect, test } from "bun:test"
import { render } from "solid-js/web"
import type { JSX } from "solid-js"
import { MaterialFacts } from "./material-facts"
import type { CaseDetailApi, 材料事实清单结果 } from "../case-detail-api"

async function 等(条件: () => boolean) {
  for (let i = 0; i < 100; i += 1) {
    if (条件()) return
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 10))
  }
  throw new Error("等超时：条件始终没成立")
}

function mount(element: () => JSX.Element) {
  const host = document.createElement("div")
  document.body.appendChild(host)
  render(element, host)
  return host
}
afterEach(() => {
  document.body.innerHTML = ""
})

const 清单: 材料事实清单结果 = {
  材料: {
    id: "m-1",
    path: "材料/话单.xlsx",
    file_name: "话单.xlsx",
    kind: "话单",
    parse_status: "解析完成",
    parse_detail: { rows: 2 },
  },
  分组: [
    {
      sheet: "Sheet#1",
      行们: [
        {
          row_no: 2,
          事实们: [
            { fact_type: "entity", fact_id: "e-张", 值: "张三", trust_state: "自动抽取" },
            { fact_type: "relation", fact_id: "r-1", 值: "张三 → 李四 通话", trust_state: "自动抽取" },
          ],
        },
      ],
    },
    { sheet: "通话明细", 行们: [] },
  ],
  缺口: ["这份材料有 3 行没抽成事实"],
}

function 假api(over: Partial<CaseDetailApi> = {}): CaseDetailApi {
  return {
    profile: async () => ({ kind: "error", message: "本用例不取档案" }),
    materialFacts: async () => ({ kind: "ok", value: 清单 }),
    timeline: async () => ({ kind: "ok", value: { 数据: [] } }),
    money: async () => ({ kind: "ok", value: { 数据: {} } }),
    graph: async () => ({ kind: "ok", value: { 节点: [], 边: [], 缺口: [] } }),
    ...over,
  }
}

describe("材料抽取结果", () => {
  test("按工作表分组，行上列出这一行的全部事实", async () => {
    const host = mount(() => <MaterialFacts projectId="p" path="材料/话单.xlsx" api={假api()} />)
    await 等(() => host.querySelectorAll("[data-slot='material-facts-sheet']").length === 2)
    const 组们 = [...host.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>("[data-slot='material-facts-sheet']")]
    expect(组们.map((组) => 组.textContent)).toEqual(["Sheet#1", "通话明细"])
    const 行 = host.querySelector<HTMLElement>("[data-slot='material-facts-row']")!
    expect(行.textContent).toContain("第 2 行")
    expect(行.textContent).toContain("张三")
    expect(行.textContent).toContain("张三 → 李四 通话")
  })

  test("空工作表与空分组都说清楚，不画一片空白", async () => {
    const host = mount(() => <MaterialFacts projectId="p" path="材料/话单.xlsx" api={假api()} />)
    await 等(() => host.textContent!.includes("通话明细"))
    expect(host.querySelectorAll("[data-slot='material-facts-empty-sheet']").length).toBe(1)
  })

  test("缺口如实列出来（截断 / 未抽取的都要说）", async () => {
    const host = mount(() => <MaterialFacts projectId="p" path="材料/话单.xlsx" api={假api()} />)
    await 等(() => host.textContent!.includes("这份材料有 3 行没抽成事实"))
    expect(host.querySelectorAll("[data-slot='material-facts-gap']").length).toBe(1)
  })

  test("解析未完成时把 parse_detail 摆出来（哪一步卡住要比「未完成」有用）", async () => {
    const host = mount(() => (
      <MaterialFacts
        projectId="p"
        path="材料/话单.xlsx"
        api={假api({
          materialFacts: async () => ({
            kind: "ok",
            value: { ...清单, 材料: { ...清单.材料, parse_status: "解析失败", parse_detail: { reason: "第 3 列缺表头" } } },
          }),
        })}
      />
    ))
    await 等(() => host.querySelectorAll("[data-slot='material-facts-detail']").length === 1)
    expect(host.querySelector("[data-slot='material-facts-detail']")!.textContent).toContain("第 3 列缺表头")
  })

  test("解析完成时不摆 parse_detail（那是给失败看的，不是常驻噪音）", async () => {
    const host = mount(() => <MaterialFacts projectId="p" path="材料/话单.xlsx" api={假api()} />)
    await 等(() => host.querySelectorAll("[data-slot='material-facts-sheet']").length === 2)
    expect(host.querySelector("[data-slot='material-facts-detail']")).toBeNull()
  })

  test("取数失败如实说（404 的原文照搬）", async () => {
    const host = mount(() => (
      <MaterialFacts
        projectId="p"
        path="没有.xlsx"
        api={假api({ materialFacts: async () => ({ kind: "error", message: "这个项目里没有这份材料" }) })}
      />
    ))
    await 等(() => host.textContent!.includes("这个项目里没有这份材料"))
    expect(host.querySelector("[data-slot='material-facts-sheet']")).toBeNull()
  })

  test("一份事实都没有时给一句话，不是空壳", async () => {
    const host = mount(() => (
      <MaterialFacts
        projectId="p"
        path="材料/话单.xlsx"
        api={假api({
          materialFacts: async () => ({ kind: "ok", value: { ...清单, 分组: [], 缺口: [] } }),
        })}
      />
    ))
    await 等(() => host.textContent!.includes("这份材料还没有登记事实"))
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 5: 实现材料页**

`case-views/material-facts.tsx`（完整）：

```tsx
import { createEffect, createSignal, For, Show, type JSX } from "solid-js"
import type { CaseDetailApi, 材料事实清单结果 } from "../case-detail-api"

export interface MaterialFactsProps {
  readonly projectId: string
  readonly path: string
  readonly api: CaseDetailApi
}

/** `parse_detail` 是 JSONB，形状随解析器变：字符串原样，其余转成缩进 JSON；空值返回空串。 */
function 解详情(值: unknown): string {
  if (值 === null || 值 === undefined) return ""
  if (typeof 值 === "string") return 值
  try {
    return JSON.stringify(值, null, 2)
  } catch {
    return String(值)
  }
}

/**
 * 一份材料的抽取结果（design §8.4）。
 *
 * **按工作表分组、行上并排列事实**——民警核对的是「这一行原文有没有被抽成事实」，
 * 按事实类型平铺就丢了「同一行」这个对照关系。
 *
 * `parse_status !== "解析完成"` 时才摆 `parse_detail`：解析成功时的 detail 是机器噪声，
 * 常驻只会把真正要看的东西挤下去。
 */
export function MaterialFacts(props: MaterialFactsProps): JSX.Element {
  const [清单, set清单] = createSignal<材料事实清单结果 | undefined>()
  const [错误, set错误] = createSignal<string | undefined>()
  const [载入, set载入] = createSignal(false)

  createEffect(() => {
    const projectId = props.projectId
    const path = props.path
    const api = props.api
    set清单(undefined)
    set错误(undefined)
    set载入(true)
    let 作废 = false
    void api.materialFacts(projectId, path).then((结果) => {
      if (作废) return
      set载入(false)
      if (结果.kind === "error") {
        set错误(结果.message)
        return
      }
      set清单(结果.value)
    })
    return () => {
      作废 = true
    }
  })

  return (
    <div data-component="material-facts" class="flex min-h-0 w-full flex-1 flex-col overflow-y-auto px-3 py-2">
      <Show when={错误()}>
        {(话) => (
          <p data-slot="material-facts-error" role="alert" class="text-[13px] text-v2-state-fg-danger">
            {话()}
          </p>
        )}
      </Show>
      <Show when={载入() && 清单() === undefined}>
        <p data-slot="material-facts-loading" class="text-[13px] text-v2-text-text-muted">
          正在读取抽取结果…
        </p>
      </Show>
      <Show when={清单()}>
        {(清单) => (
          <>
            <div class="flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-2">
              <h1 data-slot="material-facts-title" class="text-[16px] font-semibold text-v2-text-text-base">
                {清单().材料.file_name || 清单().材料.path}
              </h1>
              <span class="text-[12px] text-v2-text-text-muted">{清单().材料.kind}</span>
              <span data-slot="material-facts-status" class="rounded-[4px] bg-v2-background-bg-layer-01 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[12px] text-v2-text-text-muted">
                {清单().材料.parse_status}
              </span>
            </div>
            <Show when={清单().材料.parse_status !== "解析完成" && 解详情(清单().材料.parse_detail) !== ""}>
              <pre data-slot="material-facts-detail" class="mt-2 overflow-x-auto rounded-[4px] bg-v2-background-bg-layer-01 p-2 text-[12px] text-v2-text-text-muted">
                {解详情(清单().材料.parse_detail)}
              </pre>
            </Show>

            <Show when={清单().分组.length === 0}>
              <p data-slot="material-facts-empty" class="mt-3 text-[13px] text-v2-text-text-muted">
                这份材料还没有登记事实
              </p>
            </Show>

            <For each={清单().分组}>
              {(组) => (
                <section class="mt-3">
                  <h2 data-slot="material-facts-sheet" class="text-[13px] font-semibold text-v2-text-text-base">
                    {组.sheet}
                  </h2>
                  <Show when={组.行们.length === 0}>
                    <p data-slot="material-facts-empty-sheet" class="text-[13px] text-v2-text-text-muted">
                      这一页没有登记事实
                    </p>
                  </Show>
                  <For each={组.行们}>
                    {(行) => (
                      <div data-slot="material-facts-row" data-row={行.row_no} class="flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-1 border-b border-v2-border-border-muted py-0.5 text-[13px]">
                        <span class="shrink-0 text-v2-text-text-muted">第 {行.row_no} 行</span>
                        <For each={行.事实们}>
                          {(事实) => (
                            <span data-slot="material-fact" data-type={事实.fact_type} class="rounded-[4px] bg-v2-background-bg-layer-01 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-v2-text-text-base">
                              {事实.值}
                            </span>
                          )}
                        </For>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </For>
                </section>
              )}
            </For>

            <For each={清单().缺口}>
              {(话) => (
                <p data-slot="material-facts-gap" class="mt-1 text-[12px] text-v2-text-text-muted">
                  {话}
                </p>
              )}
            </For>
          </>
        )}
      </Show>
    </div>
  )
}
```

```tsx
  test("「查看解析结果」把 case://…/material/<路径> 开进中栏（计划 7 接上了 onReviewParse）", async () => {
    setCurrentProject(undefined)
    setProjectList(undefined)
    setProjectFiles(undefined)
    const 假 = 假材料状态({ rows: [视图行({ path: "话单.csv", status: "已解析" })] })
    const { data } = 假数据源({ list: async () => [我的(私有("p1", "专案", 1))] })
    const host = 挂(data, 假.状态)
    await 冲一遍()
    // 清单要**挂载后**再写（同上面那条用例的注释：挂载时的 effect 会先清一次）。
    setProjectFiles(["话单.csv"])
    setCurrentProject({ id: "p1", name: "专案" })
    await 冲一遍()

    右键行(host, "话单.csv")
    await 冲一遍()

    // 解析是子菜单：打开方式照 `file-tree.test.tsx:2989`（pointerenter ＋ ArrowRight 两条路都派）。
    const 触发 = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>("[data-slot='context-menu-sub-trigger']")
    if (!触发) throw new Error("没有解析子菜单触发器——前提不成立")
    触发.dispatchEvent(new PointerEvent("pointerenter", { bubbles: false, pointerId: 1 }))
    触发.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent("keydown", { key: "ArrowRight", bubbles: true, cancelable: true }))
    await 等到(() => document.querySelector("[data-component='context-menu-sub-content']") !== null)

    const 项 = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>("[data-parse-action='parse-view']")
    if (!项) throw new Error("没有「查看解析结果」——前提不成立")
    for (const 类型 of ["pointerdown", "pointerup"])
      项.dispatchEvent(new PointerEvent(类型, { bubbles: true, cancelable: true, pointerId: 1, button: 0, isPrimary: true }))
    await 冲一遍()

    // 接线成立的证据：中栏多了一张标题带「抽取结果」的 tab（没注入 caseDetailApi ⇒ 内容说未接入，
    // 那是**内容层**的事；这条只钉「路径进没进中栏」）。
    expect(host.textContent).toContain("话单.csv · 抽取结果")
  })
```

```tsx
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 写时间线的失败用例**

`case-views/timeline.test.tsx`：

```tsx
import { afterEach, describe, expect, test } from "bun:test"
import { render } from "solid-js/web"
import type { JSX } from "solid-js"
import { Timeline } from "./timeline"
import { 时间文本 } from "./format"
import type { CaseDetailApi } from "../case-detail-api"

async function 等(条件: () => boolean) {
  for (let i = 0; i < 100; i += 1) {
    if (条件()) return
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 10))
  }
  throw new Error("等超时：条件始终没成立")
}
function mount(element: () => JSX.Element) {
  const host = document.createElement("div")
  document.body.appendChild(host)
  render(element, host)
  return host
}
afterEach(() => {
  document.body.innerHTML = ""
})

const 事件们 = [
  {
    id: "ev-2",
    type: "转账",
    occurred_at: 1_700_000_100,
    occurred_raw: null,
    participants: ["张三", "李四"],
    summary: "转账 100 元",
    trust_state: "自动抽取",
  },
  {
    id: "ev-1",
    type: "见面",
    occurred_at: 1_700_000_050,
    occurred_raw: null,
    participants: ["张三"],
    summary: null,
    trust_state: "自动抽取",
  },
]

function 假api(over: Partial<CaseDetailApi> = {}): CaseDetailApi {
  return {
    profile: async () => ({ kind: "error", message: "未用" }),
    materialFacts: async () => ({ kind: "error", message: "未用" }),
    timeline: async () => ({ kind: "ok", value: { 数据: 事件们, 口径: "按参与实体过滤", 缺口: ["只列了最近 50 条"] } }),
    money: async () => ({ kind: "ok", value: { 数据: {} } }),
    graph: async () => ({ kind: "ok", value: { 节点: [], 边: [], 缺口: [] } }),
    ...over,
  }
}

describe("时间线", () => {
  test("按服务端给的顺序逐条画出时间、类型、摘要与参与者", async () => {
    const host = mount(() => <Timeline projectId="p" id="e-张" api={假api()} />)
    await 等(() => host.querySelectorAll("[data-slot='timeline-event']").length === 2)
    const 行们 = [...host.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>("[data-slot='timeline-event']")]
    expect(行们[0]!.textContent).toContain(时间文本(1_700_000_100))
    expect(行们[0]!.textContent).toContain("转账 100 元")
    expect(行们[0]!.textContent).toContain("张三、李四")
    expect(行们[1]!.textContent).toContain("见面")
  })

  test("口径与缺口都摆出来（结论能不能用，取决于覆盖到哪）", async () => {
    const host = mount(() => <Timeline projectId="p" id="e-张" api={假api()} />)
    await 等(() => host.querySelectorAll("[data-slot='timeline-event']").length === 2)
    expect(host.querySelector("[data-slot='timeline-scope']")!.textContent).toContain("按参与实体过滤")
    expect(host.querySelector("[data-slot='timeline-gap']")!.textContent).toContain("只列了最近 50 条")
  })

  test("没有事件 ⇒ 说「没有事件」，不画空列表", async () => {
    const host = mount(() => (
      <Timeline projectId="p" id="e-张" api={假api({ timeline: async () => ({ kind: "ok", value: { 数据: [] } }) })} />
    ))
    await 等(() => host.textContent!.includes("没有事件"))
    expect(host.querySelectorAll("[data-slot='timeline-event']").length).toBe(0)
  })

  test("取数失败如实说", async () => {
    const host = mount(() => (
      <Timeline projectId="p" id="e-张" api={假api({ timeline: async () => ({ kind: "error", message: "找不到这个实体" }) })} />
    ))
    await 等(() => host.textContent!.includes("找不到这个实体"))
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 5: 实现时间线**

`case-views/timeline.tsx`（完整）：

```tsx
import { createEffect, createSignal, For, Show, type JSX } from "solid-js"
import type { CaseDetailApi, 工具结果 } from "../case-detail-api"
import { 时间文本 } from "./format"

export interface TimelineProps {
  readonly projectId: string
  readonly id: string
  readonly api: CaseDetailApi
}

interface 时间线事件 {
  readonly id: string
  readonly type: string
  readonly occurred_at: number | null
  readonly participants: readonly string[]
  readonly summary: string | null
}

/** `数据` 是 `unknown`（工具结果的口径）：认不出的行整条丢掉，不猜字段。 */
function 解事件们(数据: unknown): 时间线事件[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(数据)) return []
  return 数据.flatMap((条) => {
    if (条 === null || typeof 条 !== "object") return []
    const 记 = 条 as Record<string, unknown>
    if (typeof 记.id !== "string" || typeof 记.type !== "string") return []
    return [
      {
        id: 记.id,
        type: 记.type,
        occurred_at: typeof 记.occurred_at === "number" ? 记.occurred_at : null,
        participants: Array.isArray(记.participants) ? 记.participants.filter((值): 值 is string => typeof 值 === "string") : [],
        summary: typeof 记.summary === "string" ? 记.summary : null,
      },
    ]
  })
}

/**
 * 实体时间线。**复用 `query_events` 的返回**（口径与缺口一起摆），
 * 前端不自己拼「某实体的全部事件」——那等于给 §9.1 的过滤开第二个会漂的点。
 */
export function Timeline(props: TimelineProps): JSX.Element {
  const [结果, set结果] = createSignal<工具结果 | undefined>()
  const [错误, set错误] = createSignal<string | undefined>()
  const [载入, set载入] = createSignal(false)

  createEffect(() => {
    const projectId = props.projectId
    const id = props.id
    const api = props.api
    set结果(undefined)
    set错误(undefined)
    set载入(true)
    let 作废 = false
    void api.timeline(projectId, id).then((回) => {
      if (作废) return
      set载入(false)
      if (回.kind === "error") {
        set错误(回.message)
        return
      }
      set结果(回.value)
    })
    return () => {
      作废 = true
    }
  })

  const 事件们 = () => 解事件们(结果()?.数据)

  return (
    <div data-component="timeline" class="flex min-h-0 w-full flex-1 flex-col overflow-y-auto px-3 py-2">
      <h1 class="text-[16px] font-semibold text-v2-text-text-base">时间线</h1>
      <Show when={错误()}>{(话) => <p data-slot="timeline-error" role="alert" class="text-[13px] text-v2-state-fg-danger">{话()}</p>}</Show>
      <Show when={载入() && 结果() === undefined}>
        <p class="text-[13px] text-v2-text-text-muted">正在读取事件…</p>
      </Show>
      <Show when={结果() && 事件们().length === 0 && 错误() === undefined}>
        <p data-slot="timeline-empty" class="text-[13px] text-v2-text-text-muted">没有事件</p>
      </Show>
      <For each={事件们()}>
        {(事件) => (
          <div data-slot="timeline-event" data-event-id={事件.id} class="border-b border-v2-border-border-muted py-1 text-[13px]">
            <div class="text-v2-text-text-base">
              {时间文本(事件.occurred_at)} · {事件.type}
            </div>
            <Show when={事件.summary}>{(话) => <div class="text-v2-text-text-muted">{话()}</div>}</Show>
            <div class="text-[12px] text-v2-text-text-muted">{事件.participants.join("、")}</div>
          </div>
        )}
      </For>
      <Show when={结果()?.口径}>
        {(话) => <p data-slot="timeline-scope" class="mt-2 text-[12px] text-v2-text-text-muted">{话()}</p>}
      </Show>
      <For each={结果()?.缺口 ?? []}>{(话) => <p data-slot="timeline-gap" class="text-[12px] text-v2-text-text-muted">{话}</p>}</For>
    </div>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import { afterEach, describe, expect, test } from "bun:test"
import { render } from "solid-js/web"
import type { JSX } from "solid-js"
import { Money } from "./money"
import type { CaseDetailApi } from "../case-detail-api"

async function 等(条件: () => boolean) {
  for (let i = 0; i < 100; i += 1) {
    if (条件()) return
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 10))
  }
  throw new Error("等超时：条件始终没成立")
}
function mount(element: () => JSX.Element) {
  const host = document.createElement("div")
  document.body.appendChild(host)
  render(element, host)
  return host
}
afterEach(() => {
  document.body.innerHTML = ""
})

const 数据 = {
  汇总: [
    { 方向: "出", 笔数: 3, 合计: "300.00" },
    { 方向: "入", 笔数: 1, 合计: "100.00" },
  ],
  对手方: [
    { 对手方: "6222****0001", 方向: "出", 笔数: 2, 合计: "200.00" },
    { 对手方: "李四", 方向: "入", 笔数: 1, 合计: "100.00" },
  ],
  余额走势: { 首: "1000.00", 末: "800.00", 最高: "1100.00", 最低: "800.00", 行数: 4 },
}

function 假api(over: Partial<CaseDetailApi> = {}): CaseDetailApi {
  return {
    profile: async () => ({ kind: "error", message: "未用" }),
    materialFacts: async () => ({ kind: "error", message: "未用" }),
    timeline: async () => ({ kind: "ok", value: { 数据: [] } }),
    money: async () => ({ kind: "ok", value: { 数据, 口径: "只统计已抽成关系的转账", 缺口: ["对手方最多 50 条"] } }),
    graph: async () => ({ kind: "ok", value: { 节点: [], 边: [], 缺口: [] } }),
    ...over,
  }
}

describe("资金", () => {
  test("汇总按出 / 入两行给出笔数与合计（numeric 字符串原样显示）", async () => {
    const host = mount(() => <Money projectId="p" id="e-账" api={假api()} />)
    await 等(() => host.querySelectorAll("[data-slot='money-summary']").length === 2)
    const 行们 = [...host.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>("[data-slot='money-summary']")]
    expect(行们[0]!.textContent).toContain("出")
    expect(行们[0]!.textContent).toContain("3 笔")
    expect(行们[0]!.textContent).toContain("300.00")
    expect(行们[1]!.textContent).toContain("入")
  })

  test("对手方逐条列出，方向与合计都在", async () => {
    const host = mount(() => <Money projectId="p" id="e-账" api={假api()} />)
    await 等(() => host.querySelectorAll("[data-slot='money-counterparty']").length === 2)
    const 行们 = [...host.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>("[data-slot='money-counterparty']")]
    expect(行们[0]!.textContent).toContain("6222****0001")
    expect(行们[0]!.textContent).toContain("200.00")
  })

  test("余额走势给出首 / 末 / 最高 / 最低；没有余额数据时说清楚", async () => {
    const host = mount(() => <Money projectId="p" id="e-账" api={假api()} />)
    await 等(() => host.querySelectorAll("[data-slot='money-balance']").length === 1)
    const 块 = host.querySelector("[data-slot='money-balance']")!
    expect(块.textContent).toContain("1000.00")
    expect(块.textContent).toContain("800.00")

    const 空 = mount(() => (
      <Money projectId="p" id="e-账" api={假api({ money: async () => ({ kind: "ok", value: { 数据: { ...数据, 余额走势: null } } }) })} />
    ))
    await 等(() => 空.textContent!.includes("没有可用余额"))
  })

  test("口径与缺口都摆出来", async () => {
    const host = mount(() => <Money projectId="p" id="e-账" api={假api()} />)
    await 等(() => host.querySelectorAll("[data-slot='money-summary']").length === 2)
    expect(host.querySelector("[data-slot='money-scope']")!.textContent).toContain("只统计已抽成关系的转账")
    expect(host.querySelector("[data-slot='money-gap']")!.textContent).toContain("对手方最多 50 条")
  })

  test("取数失败如实说", async () => {
    const host = mount(() => (
      <Money projectId="p" id="e-账" api={假api({ money: async () => ({ kind: "error", message: "找不到这个实体" }) })} />
    ))
    await 等(() => host.textContent!.includes("找不到这个实体"))
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 8: 实现资金页**

`case-views/money.tsx`（完整）：

```tsx
import { createEffect, createSignal, For, Show, type JSX } from "solid-js"
import type { CaseDetailApi, 工具结果 } from "../case-detail-api"
import { 金额文本 } from "./format"

export interface MoneyProps {
  readonly projectId: string
  readonly id: string
  readonly api: CaseDetailApi
}

interface 汇总行 { readonly 方向: string; readonly 笔数: number; readonly 合计: string }
interface 对手方行 { readonly 对手方: string; readonly 方向: string; readonly 笔数: number; readonly 合计: string }
interface 余额 { readonly 首: string | null; readonly 末: string | null; readonly 最高: string | null; readonly 最低: string | null; readonly 行数: number }

const 是数 = (值: unknown): 值 is number => typeof 值 === "number"
const 是串 = (值: unknown): 值 is string => typeof 值 === "string"

function 解资金(数据: unknown): { 汇总: 汇总行[]; 对手方: 对手方行[]; 余额: 余额 | null } {
  if (数据 === null || typeof 数据 !== "object") return { 汇总: [], 对手方: [], 余额: null }
  const 记 = 数据 as Record<string, unknown>
  const 汇总 = Array.isArray(记.汇总)
    ? 记.汇总.flatMap((条) => {
        if (条 === null || typeof 条 !== "object") return []
        const 行 = 条 as Record<string, unknown>
        if (!是串(行.方向) || !是数(行.笔数) || !是串(行.合计)) return []
        return [{ 方向: 行.方向, 笔数: 行.笔数, 合计: 行.合计 }]
      })
    : []
  const 对手方 = Array.isArray(记.对手方)
    ? 记.对手方.flatMap((条) => {
        if (条 === null || typeof 条 !== "object") return []
        const 行 = 条 as Record<string, unknown>
        if (!是串(行.对手方) || !是串(行.方向) || !是数(行.笔数) || !是串(行.合计)) return []
        return [{ 对手方: 行.对手方, 方向: 行.方向, 笔数: 行.笔数, 合计: 行.合计 }]
      })
    : []
  const 原余额 = 记.余额走势
  const 余额 =
    原余额 === null || typeof 原余额 !== "object"
      ? null
      : {
          首: 是串((原余额 as Record<string, unknown>).首) ? ((原余额 as Record<string, string>).首 ?? null) : null,
          末: 是串((原余额 as Record<string, unknown>).末) ? ((原余额 as Record<string, string>).末 ?? null) : null,
          最高: 是串((原余额 as Record<string, unknown>).最高) ? ((原余额 as Record<string, string>).最高 ?? null) : null,
          最低: 是串((原余额 as Record<string, unknown>).最低) ? ((原余额 as Record<string, string>).最低 ?? null) : null,
          行数: 是数((原余额 as Record<string, unknown>).行数) ? ((原余额 as Record<string, number>).行数 ?? 0) : 0,
        }
  return { 汇总, 对手方, 余额 }
}

/** 资金概览。汇总 / 对手方 / 余额都来自 `aggregate_money` 的**同一次**返回，前端不再分头取数。 */
export function Money(props: MoneyProps): JSX.Element {
  const [结果, set结果] = createSignal<工具结果 | undefined>()
  const [错误, set错误] = createSignal<string | undefined>()
  const [载入, set载入] = createSignal(false)

  createEffect(() => {
    const projectId = props.projectId
    const id = props.id
    const api = props.api
    set结果(undefined)
    set错误(undefined)
    set载入(true)
    let 作废 = false
    void api.money(projectId, id).then((回) => {
      if (作废) return
      set载入(false)
      if (回.kind === "error") {
        set错误(回.message)
        return
      }
      set结果(回.value)
    })
    return () => {
      作废 = true
    }
  })

  const 解 = () => 解资金(结果()?.数据)

  return (
    <div data-component="money" class="flex min-h-0 w-full flex-1 flex-col overflow-y-auto px-3 py-2">
      <h1 class="text-[16px] font-semibold text-v2-text-text-base">资金</h1>
      <Show when={错误()}>{(话) => <p data-slot="money-error" role="alert" class="text-[13px] text-v2-state-fg-danger">{话()}</p>}</Show>
      <Show when={载入() && 结果() === undefined}>
        <p class="text-[13px] text-v2-text-text-muted">正在汇总资金…</p>
      </Show>

      <Show when={结果()}>
        <div class="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <For each={解().汇总}>
            {(行) => (
              <div data-slot="money-summary" data-方向={行.方向} class="rounded-[4px] bg-v2-background-bg-layer-01 px-2 py-1 text-[13px] text-v2-text-text-base">
                {行.方向} {行.笔数} 笔 · {金额文本(行.合计)}
              </div>
            )}
          </For>
          <Show when={解().汇总.length === 0}>
            <p class="text-[13px] text-v2-text-text-muted">没有可汇总的转账</p>
          </Show>
        </div>

        <h2 class="mt-3 text-[13px] font-semibold text-v2-text-text-base">对手方</h2>
        <Show when={解().对手方.length === 0}>
          <p class="text-[13px] text-v2-text-text-muted">没有对手方</p>
        </Show>
        <For each={解().对手方}>
          {(行) => (
            <div data-slot="money-counterparty" class="flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-1 border-b border-v2-border-border-muted py-0.5 text-[13px]">
              <span class="text-v2-text-text-base">{行.对手方}</span>
              <span class="text-v2-text-text-muted">{行.方向}</span>
              <span class="text-v2-text-text-muted">
                {行.笔数} 笔 · {金额文本(行.合计)}
              </span>
            </div>
          )}
        </For>

        <h2 class="mt-3 text-[13px] font-semibold text-v2-text-text-base">余额</h2>
        <Show when={解().余额} fallback={<p class="text-[13px] text-v2-text-text-muted">没有可用余额</p>}>
          {(余额) => (
            <div data-slot="money-balance" class="text-[13px] text-v2-text-text-base">
              首 {金额文本(余额().首)} · 末 {金额文本(余额().末)} · 最高 {金额文本(余额().最高)} · 最低{" "}
              {金额文本(余额().最低)} · 共 {余额().行数} 行
            </div>
          )}
        </Show>
      </Show>

      <Show when={结果()?.口径}>
        {(话) => <p data-slot="money-scope" class="mt-2 text-[12px] text-v2-text-text-muted">{话()}</p>}
      </Show>
      <For each={结果()?.缺口 ?? []}>{(话) => <p data-slot="money-gap" class="text-[12px] text-v2-text-text-muted">{话}</p>}</For>
    </div>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 11: 实现关系图**

`case-views/graph.tsx`（完整）：

```tsx
import { createEffect, createMemo, createSignal, For, Show, type JSX } from "solid-js"
import type { CaseDetailApi, 图节点, 关系图结果 } from "../case-detail-api"
import { 金额文本 } from "./format"

export interface GraphProps {
  readonly projectId: string
  readonly id: string
  readonly api: CaseDetailApi
  readonly onOpenEntity: (点: 图节点) => void
}

const 宽 = 640
const 高 = 420
const 中心点 = { x: 宽 / 2, y: 高 / 2 }
const 一层半径 = 150
const 二层半径 = 250

/**
 * 环状布局：中心固定，1 跳排内环、2 跳排外环，**按 id 排序**保证同一份数据每次画得一样
 * （民警截图对照时，位置跳来跳去会被当成「图变了」）。不引图形库：这张图只服务 50 个点。
 */
function 布局(图: 关系图结果) {
  const 排 = (点们: readonly 图节点[], 半径: number) => {
    const 有序 = [...点们].sort((甲, 乙) => 甲.id.localeCompare(乙.id))
    return 有序.map((点, 序号) => {
      const 角 = (Math.PI * 2 * 序号) / Math.max(有序.length, 1) - Math.PI / 2
      return { ...点, x: 中心点.x + Math.cos(角) * 半径, y: 中心点.y + Math.sin(角) * 半径 }
    })
  }
  const 中心 = 图.节点.filter((点) => 点.中心)
  const 一层 = 图.节点.filter((点) => 点.跳数 === 1)
  const 二层 = 图.节点.filter((点) => 点.跳数 === 2)
  return {
    中心: 中心.map((点) => ({ ...点, x: 中心点.x, y: 中心点.y })),
    一层: 排(一层, 一层半径),
    二层: 排(二层, 二层半径),
  }
}

export function Graph(props: GraphProps): JSX.Element {
  const [图, set图] = createSignal<关系图结果 | undefined>()
  const [错误, set错误] = createSignal<string | undefined>()
  const [载入, set载入] = createSignal(false)
  const [跳数, set跳数] = createSignal<1 | 2>(1)
  const [选中类型, set选中类型] = createSignal<readonly string[]>([])

  createEffect(() => {
    const projectId = props.projectId
    const id = props.id
    const api = props.api
    const 当前跳数 = 跳数()
    const 当前类型 = 选中类型()
    set图(undefined)
    set错误(undefined)
    set载入(true)
    let 作废 = false
    void api.graph(projectId, id, 当前跳数, 当前类型).then((回) => {
      if (作废) return
      set载入(false)
      if (回.kind === "error") {
        set错误(回.message)
        return
      }
      set图(回.value)
    })
    return () => {
      作废 = true
    }
  })

  const 位置 = createMemo(() => (图() ? 布局(图()!) : { 中心: [], 一层: [], 二层: [] }))
  const 全部点 = createMemo(() => {
    const 位 = 位置()
    return [...位.中心, ...位.一层, ...位.二层]
  })
  const 点 = (id: string) => 全部点().find((行) => 行.id === id)

  /** 类型筛选的候选项来自**当前返回的边**（没出现的类型不摆，免得点了没变化）。 */
  const 类型们 = createMemo(() => [...new Set((图()?.边 ?? []).map((边) => 边.type))].sort())

  const 拨类型 = (类型: string) => {
    const 当前 = 选中类型()
    set选中类型(当前.includes(类型) ? 当前.filter((条) => 条 !== 类型) : [...当前, 类型])
  }

  return (
    <div data-component="graph" class="flex min-h-0 w-full flex-1 flex-col overflow-y-auto px-3 py-2">
      <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <h1 class="text-[16px] font-semibold text-v2-text-text-base">关系图</h1>
        <For each={[1, 2] as const}>
          {(层) => (
            <button
              type="button"
              data-slot="graph-hop"
              data-hop={层}
              aria-pressed={跳数() === 层}
              onClick={() => set跳数(层)}
              class={跳数() === 层 ? "h-6 rounded-[4px] bg-[var(--v2-background-bg-accent-soft)] px-2 text-[12px] font-semibold" : "h-6 rounded-[4px] px-2 text-[12px] text-v2-text-text-muted"}
            >
              {层} 跳
            </button>
          )}
        </For>
        <For each={类型们()}>
          {(类型) => (
            <button
              type="button"
              data-slot="graph-type"
              data-type={类型}
              aria-pressed={选中类型().includes(类型)}
              onClick={() => 拨类型(类型)}
              class={选中类型().includes(类型) ? "h-6 rounded-[4px] bg-[var(--v2-background-bg-accent-soft)] px-2 text-[12px] font-semibold" : "h-6 rounded-[4px] px-2 text-[12px] text-v2-text-text-muted"}
            >
              {类型}
            </button>
          )}
        </For>
      </div>

      <Show when={错误()}>{(话) => <p data-slot="graph-error" role="alert" class="text-[13px] text-v2-state-fg-danger">{话()}</p>}</Show>
      <Show when={载入() && 图() === undefined}>
        <p class="text-[13px] text-v2-text-text-muted">正在读取关系…</p>
      </Show>
      <Show when={图() && 图()!.边.length === 0 && 错误() === undefined}>
        <p data-slot="graph-empty" class="text-[13px] text-v2-text-text-muted">还没有关系</p>
      </Show>

      <Show when={图() && 图()!.边.length > 0}>
        <svg viewBox={`0 0 ${宽} ${高}`} class="mt-2 w-full max-w-[720px] self-start">
          <For each={图()!.边}>
            {(边) => {
              const 甲 = () => 点(边.from)
              const 乙 = () => 点(边.to)
              return (
                <Show when={甲() && 乙()}>
                  {() => (
                    <line
                      data-slot="graph-edge"
                      data-edge-id={边.id}
                      x1={甲()!.x}
                      y1={甲()!.y}
                      x2={乙()!.x}
                      y2={乙()!.y}
                      stroke="currentColor"
                      stroke-opacity="0.35"
                    />
                  )}
                </Show>
              )
            }}
          </For>
          <For each={全部点()}>
            {(点) => (
              <g
                data-slot="graph-node"
                data-node-id={点.id}
                data-hop={点.跳数}
                data-center={点.中心 ? "true" : "false"}
                class="cursor-pointer"
                onClick={() => props.onOpenEntity(点)}
              >
                <circle cx={点.x} cy={点.y} r={点.中心 ? 16 : 11} fill={点.中心 ? "var(--v2-background-bg-accent-soft)" : "var(--v2-background-bg-layer-01)"} stroke="currentColor" stroke-opacity="0.5" />
                <text x={点.x} y={点.y + (点.中心 ? 32 : 26)} text-anchor="middle" class="fill-current text-[11px]">
                  {点.value}
                </text>
              </g>
            )}
          </For>
        </svg>
        <For each={图()!.边}>
          {(边) => (
            <p data-slot="graph-edge-label" class="text-[12px] text-v2-text-text-muted">
              {点(边.from)?.value ?? 边.from} —{边.type}
              {边.times === null ? "" : ` ${边.times} 次`}
              {边.amount === null ? "" : ` · ${金额文本(边.amount)}`}— {点(边.to)?.value ?? 边.to}
            </p>
          )}
        </For>
      </Show>

      <For each={图()?.缺口 ?? []}>{(话) => <p data-slot="graph-gap" class="mt-1 text-[12px] text-v2-text-text-muted">{话}</p>}</For>
    </div>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import type { CaseDetailApi, 图节点 } from "./case-detail-api"
import { 案件伪路径, 解析案件路径, type 案件视图, type 案件路径 } from "./case-route"
import { EntityProfile } from "./case-views/entity-profile"
import { Graph } from "./case-views/graph"
import { MaterialFacts } from "./case-views/material-facts"
import { Money } from "./case-views/money"
import { Timeline } from "./case-views/timeline"

export interface CaseViewProps {
  readonly path: string
  readonly api?: CaseDetailApi
  readonly open?: (input: { readonly title: string; readonly path: string }) => void
}

…
            {(api) => 视图(路径(), api(), props.open)}
…
function 视图(
  路径: 案件路径,
  api: CaseDetailApi,
  open: CaseViewProps["open"],
): JSX.Element {
  /** 同一实体换视图：arg 一律是实体 id；标题由点按钮的那一层给。 */
  const 跳到 = (view: 案件视图, title: string) =>
    open?.({ title, path: 案件伪路径({ projectId: 路径.projectId, view, arg: 路径.arg }) })

  switch (路径.view) {
    case "entity":
      return (
        <EntityProfile
          projectId={路径.projectId}
          id={路径.arg}
          api={api}
          onOpen={(view, 标题) => 跳到(view, 标题)}
        />
      )
    case "material":
      return <MaterialFacts projectId={路径.projectId} path={路径.arg} api={api} />
    case "timeline":
      return <Timeline projectId={路径.projectId} id={路径.arg} api={api} />
    case "money":
      return <Money projectId={路径.projectId} id={路径.arg} api={api} />
    case "graph":
      return (
        <Graph
          projectId={路径.projectId}
          id={路径.arg}
          api={api}
          onOpenEntity={(点: 图节点) =>
            open?.({
              title: `${点.value} · ${点.type}`,
              path: 案件伪路径({ projectId: 路径.projectId, view: "entity", arg: 点.id }),
            })
          }
        />
      )
  }
}
```

```ts
// case-alerts.ts
export interface 提醒行 {
  readonly id: string
  readonly rule_id: string
  readonly 标题: string
  readonly 内容: string
  readonly 事实: unknown
  readonly created_at: number
  readonly read_at: number | null
  readonly handled_at: number | null
}
export const 规则 = {
  资金对称: "资金对称",
  通话转账重合: "通话转账重合",
  号码多账户: "号码多账户",
} as const
export const 金额对称偏差 = 0.05
export const 对称最少笔数 = 2
export const 对称最少金额 = 5000
export const 通话转账最少金额 = 10000
export const 号码最少账户 = 3
export async function 生成提醒(db: Queryable, projectId: string): Promise<{ 新建: number; 提醒: 提醒行[] }>
export async function 提醒清单(db: Queryable, projectId: string): Promise<提醒行[]>
export async function 标记已读(db: Queryable, projectId: string, ids: readonly string[]): Promise<number>

// HTTP
GET  /openhive/case/alerts?projectId            → { 提醒: 提醒行[] }
POST /openhive/case/alerts/refresh  {projectId} → { 新建: number; 提醒: 提醒行[] }
POST /openhive/case/alerts/read     {projectId, ids} → { 已读: number }

// 前端
export interface CaseAlertsApi {
  readonly list: (projectId: string) => Promise<结果<提醒行[]>>
  readonly refresh: (projectId: string) => Promise<结果<{ 新建: number; 提醒: 提醒行[] }>>
  readonly markRead: (projectId: string, ids: readonly string[]) => Promise<结果<{ 已读: number }>>
}
export function createCaseAlertsApi(send?: CaseFetch): CaseAlertsApi
export function 提醒路径(projectId: string): string   // `case://<id>/alerts/all`
export function CaseAlertEntry(props: { projectId: () => string | undefined; api?: CaseAlertsApi; onOpen: () => void }): JSX.Element
export function CaseAlertEntryConnected(): JSX.Element
export function Alerts(props: { projectId: string; api: CaseDetailApi; 提醒api: CaseAlertsApi }): JSX.Element
```

```ts
  ["r-入1", "e-甲", "e-账"], ["r-入2", "e-乙", "e-账"],
  ["r-出1", "e-账", "e-丙"], ["r-出2", "e-账", "e-丁"],
] as const)
  await insertRelation(库(), { id, projectId: 项目, fromId: from, toId: to, type: "转账", amount: "1000.00" })

// R2 命中：e-张 ↔ e-李 既有通话又有转账（合计 12000 ≥ 10000）
await insertRelation(库(), { id: "r-张李话", projectId: 项目, fromId: "e-张", toId: "e-李", type: "通话" })
await insertRelation(库(), { id: "r-张李转", projectId: 项目, fromId: "e-张", toId: "e-李", type: "转账", amount: "12000.00" })

// R3 命中：e-号 连到 3 个账户
for (const [id, 账] of [["r-号1", "e-账1"], ["r-号2", "e-账2"], ["r-号3", "e-账3"]] as const)
  await insertRelation(库(), { id, projectId: 项目, fromId: "e-号", toId: 账, type: "实名登记" })

// 不该命中的噪声：已排除的关系不算
await insertRelation(库(), {
  id: "r-排", projectId: 项目, fromId: "e-甲", toId: "e-账", type: "转账", amount: "1000.00", trustState: "已排除",
})
```

用例：

```ts
test("三条规则各自命中，且已排除的关系不算", async () => {
  const { 提醒 } = await 生成提醒(库(), 项目)
  expect(提醒.map((条) => 条.rule_id).sort()).toEqual(["号码多账户", "资金对称", "通话转账重合"].sort())
  const 对称 = 提醒.find((条) => 条.rule_id === 规则.资金对称)!
  expect(对称.事实).toMatchObject({ 账户: "e-账", 出笔: 2, 入笔: 2 })
})

test("重复生成幂等：第二次 新建 = 0，清单长度不变", async () => {
  await 生成提醒(库(), 项目)
  const 第二次 = await 生成提醒(库(), 项目)
  expect(第二次.新建).toBe(0)
  expect((await 提醒清单(库(), 项目)).length).toBe(3)
})

test("已读状态在重复生成后保住（提醒不是每次都「又变成新的一批」）", async () => {
  const 清单 = await 提醒清单(库(), 项目)
  expect(await 标记已读(库(), 项目, [清单[0]!.id])).toBe(1)
  await 生成提醒(库(), 项目)
  const 之后 = await 提醒清单(库(), 项目)
  expect(之后.find((条) => 条.id === 清单[0]!.id)!.read_at).not.toBeNull()
})

test("标记已读只认本项目的 id（别的项目的 id 改不到）", async () => {
  const 别人的 = await 生成提醒(库(), 别人的项目)
  expect(await 标记已读(库(), 项目, 别人的.提醒.map((条) => 条.id))).toBe(0)
})
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 跑，确认红**（`Cannot find module ".../case-alerts"`）

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现 DAO**

`case-alerts.ts`（完整）：

```ts
/**
 * 主动提醒（design §7.3）。
 *
 * **提醒只陈述数据事实，结论由人下**：文案里不出现「可疑」「涉嫌」，只写「出 N 笔 A 元 / 入 M 笔 B 元」。
 *
 * 三条规则都是**全量比对**（每次 `生成提醒` 扫全项目），不是增量：本计划的触发时机是
 * 用户点「重新比对」＋ 抽完一批材料后由调用方触发；定时常驻留给后续计划。
 *
 * 幂等靠**确定性 id**（`al-` + `Hash.fast(projectId|ruleId|key)`）＋ `on conflict do nothing`：
 * 同一份数据重复比对不会堆出重复提醒，已读状态也不会被冲掉（`LEARNINGS #007-01`）。
 */
import { Hash } from "@opencode-ai/core/util/hash"
import { rowsOf } from "@opencode-ai/auth/migrate"
import { nowSeconds } from "@opencode-ai/auth/time"
import { sql } from "drizzle-orm"
import type { Queryable } from "./case-store"

export interface 提醒行 {
  readonly id: string
  readonly rule_id: string
  readonly 标题: string
  readonly 内容: string
  readonly 事实: unknown
  readonly created_at: number
  readonly read_at: number | null
  readonly handled_at: number | null
}

export const 规则 = {
  资金对称: "资金对称",
  通话转账重合: "通话转账重合",
  号码多账户: "号码多账户",
} as const

/** 出 / 入合计的**相对**偏差上限（5%）。 */
export const 金额对称偏差 = 0.05
export const 对称最少笔数 = 2
export const 对称最少金额 = 5000
export const 通话转账最少金额 = 10000
export const 号码最少账户 = 3

interface 待写 {
  readonly rule_id: string
  readonly key: string
  readonly 标题: string
  readonly 内容: string
  readonly 事实: unknown
}

const 提醒ID = (projectId: string, 条: 待写) =>
  `al-${Hash.fast(`${projectId}|${条.rule_id}|${条.key}`)}`

/** 规整金额显示：numeric 字符串原样（不转浮点），只在比值计算时用 `Number`。 */
const 金额 = (值: unknown) => (typeof 值 === "string" || typeof 值 === "number" ? String(值) : "0")

async function 资金对称(db: Queryable, projectId: string): Promise<待写[]> {
  const 行们 = rowsOf(
    await db.execute(sql`
      with 边 as (
        select r.from_id as 端, r.to_id as 对方, r.amount::numeric as 金额, '出' as 方向
          from auth.case_relation r
         where r.project_id = ${projectId} and r.type = '转账'
           and r.trust_state <> '已排除' and r.amount is not null
           and exists (select 1 from auth.case_entity e2
                        where e2.project_id = r.project_id and e2.id = r.to_id and e2.trust_state <> '已排除')
        union all
        select r.to_id, r.from_id, r.amount::numeric, '入'
          from auth.case_relation r
         where r.project_id = ${projectId} and r.type = '转账'
           and r.trust_state <> '已排除' and r.amount is not null
           and exists (select 1 from auth.case_entity e2
                        where e2.project_id = r.project_id and e2.id = r.from_id and e2.trust_state <> '已排除')
      )
      select 边.端 as id, 实体.canonical_value as 值, 实体.type as 类型,
             sum(case when 方向 = '出' then 金额 else 0 end)::text as 出计,
             count(*) filter (where 方向 = '出')::int as 出笔,
             sum(case when 方向 = '入' then 金额 else 0 end)::text as 入计,
             count(*) filter (where 方向 = '入')::int as 入笔
        from 边
        join auth.case_entity 实体
          on 实体.project_id = ${projectId} and 实体.id = 边.端 and 实体.trust_state <> '已排除'
       group by 边.端, 实体.canonical_value, 实体.type
      having count(*) filter (where 方向 = '出') >= ${对称最少笔数}
         and count(*) filter (where 方向 = '入') >= ${对称最少笔数}
    `),
  )
  return 行们.flatMap((行) => {
    const 出计 = Number(行.出计)
    const 入计 = Number(行.入计)
    if (出计 < 对称最少金额 || 入计 < 对称最少金额) return []
    if (Math.abs(出计 - 入计) / Math.max(出计, 入计) > 金额对称偏差) return []
    const 出笔 = Number(行.出笔)
    const 入笔 = Number(行.入笔)
    return [
      {
        rule_id: 规则.资金对称,
        key: String(行.id),
        标题: `${String(行.值)} 短期资金进出高度对称`,
        内容:
          `${String(行.类型)}「${String(行.值)}」共出 ${出笔} 笔 ${金额(行.出计)} 元、` +
          `入 ${入笔} 笔 ${金额(行.入计)} 元；两向合计相差不超过 ${Math.round(金额对称偏差 * 100)}%。`,
        事实: { 账户: 行.id, 值: 行.值, 类型: 行.类型, 出笔, 入笔, 出计: 行.出计, 入计: 行.入计 },
      },
    ]
  })
}

async function 通话转账重合(db: Queryable, projectId: string): Promise<待写[]> {
  const 行们 = rowsOf(
    await db.execute(sql`
      select a.from_id as 甲, a.to_id as 乙,
             甲.canonical_value as 甲值, 乙.canonical_value as 乙值,
             sum(a.amount)::text as 转账合计, count(*)::int as 转账笔数
        from auth.case_relation a
        join auth.case_entity 甲 on 甲.project_id = a.project_id and 甲.id = a.from_id and 甲.trust_state <> '已排除'
        join auth.case_entity 乙 on 乙.project_id = a.project_id and 乙.id = a.to_id and 乙.trust_state <> '已排除'
       where a.project_id = ${projectId} and a.type = '转账' and a.trust_state <> '已排除'
         and exists (select 1 from auth.case_relation b
                      where b.project_id = a.project_id and b.type = '通话' and b.trust_state <> '已排除'
                        and ((b.from_id = a.from_id and b.to_id = a.to_id)
                          or (b.from_id = a.to_id and b.to_id = a.from_id)))
       group by a.from_id, a.to_id, 甲.canonical_value, 乙.canonical_value
      having sum(a.amount) >= ${通话转账最少金额}
    `),
  )
  return 行们.map((行) => {
    // 甲/乙 的 id 顺序随关系方向变；key 排成与方向无关的全序（`LEARNINGS #010-05`）。
    const [小, 大] = [String(行.甲), String(行.乙)].sort()
    return {
      rule_id: 规则.通话转账重合,
      key: `${小}|${大}`,
      标题: `${String(行.甲值)} 与 ${String(行.乙值)} 既有通话又有转账`,
      内容:
        `两人之间转账 ${Number(行.转账笔数)} 笔、合计 ${金额(行.转账合计)} 元，` +
        `同时存在通话记录。（口径：同一对实体；转账合计 ≥ ${通话转账最少金额} 元）`,
      事实: { 甲: 行.甲, 乙: 行.乙, 甲值: 行.甲值, 乙值: 行.乙值, 转账合计: 行.转账合计, 转账笔数: 行.转账笔数 },
    }
  })
}

async function 号码多账户(db: Queryable, projectId: string): Promise<待写[]> {
  const 行们 = rowsOf(
    await db.execute(sql`
      select n.id as id, n.canonical_value as 值, count(distinct 账.id)::int as 账户数
        from auth.case_entity n
        join auth.case_relation r
          on r.project_id = n.project_id and (r.from_id = n.id or r.to_id = n.id)
         and r.trust_state <> '已排除'
        join auth.case_entity 账
          on 账.project_id = r.project_id
         and 账.id = case when r.from_id = n.id then r.to_id else r.from_id end
         and 账.type = '账户' and 账.trust_state <> '已排除'
       where n.project_id = ${projectId} and n.type = '号码' and n.trust_state <> '已排除'
       group by n.id, n.canonical_value
      having count(distinct 账.id) >= ${号码最少账户}
    `),
  )
  return 行们.map((行) => ({
    rule_id: 规则.号码多账户,
    key: String(行.id),
    标题: `号码 ${String(行.值)} 关联了 ${Number(行.账户数)} 个账户`,
    内容: `号码「${String(行.值)}」在项目里关联到 ${Number(行.账户数)} 个不同账户。`,
    事实: { 号码: 行.id, 值: 行.值, 账户数: 行.账户数 },
  }))
}

export async function 生成提醒(
  db: Queryable,
  projectId: string,
): Promise<{ 新建: number; 提醒: 提醒行[] }> {
  const 待写们 = [
    ...(await 资金对称(db, projectId)),
    ...(await 通话转账重合(db, projectId)),
    ...(await 号码多账户(db, projectId)),
  ]
  const 现在 = nowSeconds()
  let 新建 = 0
  for (const 条 of 待写们) {
    const 插入 = rowsOf(
      await db.execute(sql`
        insert into auth.case_alert (id, project_id, rule_id, fact_refs, created_at)
        values (
          ${提醒ID(projectId, 条)}, ${projectId}, ${条.rule_id},
          ${JSON.stringify({ 标题: 条.标题, 内容: 条.内容, 事实: 条.事实 })}::text::jsonb,
          ${现在}
        )
        on conflict (project_id, id) do nothing
        returning id
      `),
    )
    新建 += 插入.length
  }
  return { 新建, 提醒: await 提醒清单(db, projectId) }
}

const 解JSON = <T>(值: unknown, 缺省: T): T => {
  if (typeof 值 !== "string") return 缺省
  try {
    return JSON.parse(值) as T
  } catch {
    return 缺省
  }
}

export async function 提醒清单(db: Queryable, projectId: string): Promise<提醒行[]> {
  return rowsOf(
    await db.execute(sql`
      select id, rule_id, fact_refs::text as fact_refs, created_at, read_at, handled_at
        from auth.case_alert
       where project_id = ${projectId}
       order by created_at desc, id asc
    `),
  ).map((行) => {
    const 体 = 解JSON<{ 标题?: string; 内容?: string; 事实?: unknown }>(行.fact_refs, {})
    return {
      id: String(行.id),
      rule_id: String(行.rule_id),
      标题: 体.标题 ?? String(行.rule_id),
      内容: 体.内容 ?? "",
      事实: 体.事实 ?? null,
      created_at: Number(行.created_at),
      read_at: 行.read_at === null ? null : Number(行.read_at),
      handled_at: 行.handled_at === null ? null : Number(行.handled_at),
    }
  })
}

export async function 标记已读(db: Queryable, projectId: string, ids: readonly string[]): Promise<number> {
  const 有效 = ids.filter((id) => typeof id === "string" && id !== "").slice(0, 200)
  if (有效.length === 0) return 0
  const 行们 = rowsOf(
    await db.execute(sql`
      update auth.case_alert
         set read_at = ${nowSeconds()}
       where project_id = ${projectId} and read_at is null
         and id in (${sql.join(有效.map((id) => sql`${id}`), sql`, `)})
      returning id
    `),
  )
  return 行们.length
}
```

```ts
  expect((await 请求(`/openhive/case/alerts?projectId=${项目}`, { cookie: 别人的Cookie })).status).toBe(403)
```

> 提醒的种子：`refresh` 依赖案件关系；本文件 `beforeAll` 已有 `r-路由`（转账 1000、2 次）——**未必命中任何一条规则**（`资金对称` 需要双向 ≥2 笔且 ≥5000；`通话转账重合` 需要同一对实体还有通话；`号码多账户` 需要 3 个账户）。所以要先在 `beforeAll` 补 R1 的种子（照 Task 9 Step 1 的 `e-甲/e-乙/e-丙/e-丁` 四笔 1000），否则 `新建 > 0` 那条会红在「没种子」而不是「没实现」。补齐后再跑。

- [ ] **Step 5: 实现三个出口**

`case-ui-routes.ts`：

1. `PATH` 加：
```ts
  alerts: `${PREFIX}/alerts`,
  alertsRefresh: `${PREFIX}/alerts/refresh`,
  alertsRead: `${PREFIX}/alerts/read`,
```
2. import：
```ts
import { 生成提醒, 提醒清单, 标记已读 } from "./case-alerts"
```
3. `routes` 里注册：
```ts
    yield* router.add("GET", PATH.alerts, (request) => handleAlerts(request, deps))
    yield* router.add("POST", PATH.alertsRefresh, (request) => handleAlertsRefresh(request, deps))
    yield* router.add("POST", PATH.alertsRead, (request) => handleAlertsRead(request, deps))
```
4. 读体（若 Task 4 没引入）：
```ts
/** 体不是 JSON 对象 ⇒ `undefined`（同 `case-fact-routes.ts:81`）。 */
function 读体(request: HttpServerRequest.HttpServerRequest) {
  return request.json.pipe(
    Effect.match({ onFailure: () => undefined, onSuccess: (value: unknown) => (isRecord(value) ? value : undefined) }),
  )
}
const isRecord = (value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> =>
  typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value)
```
5. 三个 handler：

```ts
function handleAlerts(request: HttpServerRequest.HttpServerRequest, deps: Deps) {
  return Effect.gen(function* () {
    const 参数 = new URL(request.url, "http://localhost").searchParams
    const projectId = 参数.get("projectId")
    const 门 = yield* 过门(request, deps, projectId)
    if (是响应(门)) return 门
    return ok({ 提醒: yield* Effect.promise(() => 提醒清单(deps.pg(), projectId!)) })
  })
}

function handleAlertsRefresh(request: HttpServerRequest.HttpServerRequest, deps: Deps) {
  return Effect.gen(function* () {
    const body = yield* 读体(request)
    const projectId = typeof body?.projectId === "string" ? body.projectId : null
    const 门 = yield* 过门(request, deps, projectId)
    if (是响应(门)) return 门
    return ok(yield* Effect.promise(() => 生成提醒(deps.pg(), projectId!)))
  })
}

function handleAlertsRead(request: HttpServerRequest.HttpServerRequest, deps: Deps) {
  return Effect.gen(function* () {
    const body = yield* 读体(request)
    const projectId = typeof body?.projectId === "string" ? body.projectId : null
    const 门 = yield* 过门(request, deps, projectId)
    if (是响应(门)) return 门
    const ids = body?.ids
    if (!Array.isArray(ids) || ids.some((id) => typeof id !== "string" || id === ""))
      return fail(400, "ids 必须是字符串数组", "bad_request")
    return ok({ 已读: yield* Effect.promise(() => 标记已读(deps.pg(), projectId!, ids as string[])) })
  })
}
```

`case-alerts-api.test.ts`：

```ts
import { describe, expect, test } from "bun:test"
import { createCaseAlertsApi, 提醒路径 } from "./case-alerts-api"

const 回包 = (body: unknown, status = 200) =>
  new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { "content-type": "application/json" } })

describe("提醒 API 客户端", () => {
  test("list 读 query，refresh / read 走 POST + JSON 体", async () => {
    const 看到: Array<{ url: string; init?: RequestInit }> = []
    const api = createCaseAlertsApi(async (url, init) => {
      看到.push({ url, init })
      if (url.includes("/refresh")) return 回包({ 新建: 2, 提醒: [] })
      if (url.includes("/read")) return 回包({ 已读: 1 })
      return 回包({ 提醒: [] })
    })
    await api.list("p 1")
    await api.refresh("p 1")
    await api.markRead("p 1", ["al-1"])
    expect(看到[0]!.url).toBe("/openhive/case/alerts?projectId=p+1")
    expect(看到[1]!.init?.method).toBe("POST")
    expect(JSON.parse(String(看到[2]!.init?.body))).toEqual({ projectId: "p 1", ids: ["al-1"] })
  })

  test("非 2xx 收成 error，不抛", async () => {
    const api = createCaseAlertsApi(async () => 回包({ error: "不是该项目成员" }, 403))
    expect(await api.list("p")).toEqual({ kind: "error", message: "不是该项目成员", code: undefined })
  })

  test("提醒路径：alerts/all 也走伪路径编码", () => {
    expect(提醒路径("p 1")).toBe("case://p%201/alerts/all")
  })
})
```

`case-alert-entry.test.tsx`：

```tsx
import { afterEach, describe, expect, test } from "bun:test"
import { render } from "solid-js/web"
import type { JSX } from "solid-js"
import { CaseAlertEntry } from "./case-alert-entry"
import type { CaseAlertsApi, 提醒行 } from "./case-alerts-api"

async function 等(条件: () => boolean) {
  for (let i = 0; i < 100; i += 1) {
    if (条件()) return
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 10))
  }
  throw new Error("等超时：条件始终没成立")
}
function mount(element: () => JSX.Element) {
  const host = document.createElement("div")
  document.body.appendChild(host)
  render(element, host)
  return host
}
afterEach(() => {
  document.body.innerHTML = ""
})

const 未读: 提醒行 = {
  id: "al-1", rule_id: "资金对称", 标题: "张三 短期资金进出高度对称", 内容: "出 2 笔、入 2 笔",
  事实: null, created_at: 1, read_at: null, handled_at: null,
}

function 假api(over: Partial<CaseAlertsApi> = {}): CaseAlertsApi {
  return {
    list: async () => ({ kind: "ok", value: [未读] }),
    refresh: async () => ({ kind: "ok", value: { 新建: 1, 提醒: [未读] } }),
    markRead: async () => ({ kind: "ok", value: { 已读: 1 } }),
    ...over,
  }
}

describe("右栏提醒入口", () => {
  test("有未读 ⇒ 「新提醒 1 条」，并带 data-unread", async () => {
    const host = mount(() => <CaseAlertEntry projectId={() => "p"} api={假api()} onOpen={() => {}} />)
    await 等(() => host.querySelector("[data-slot='case-alert-count']") !== null)
    expect(host.querySelector("[data-slot='case-alert-count']")!.textContent).toContain("新提醒 1 条")
    expect(host.querySelector("[data-slot='case-alert-entry']")!.getAttribute("data-unread")).toBe("1")
  })

  test("全部已读 ⇒ 说「没有新提醒」，入口仍在（还能点进清单看历史）", async () => {
    const host = mount(() => (
      <CaseAlertEntry projectId={() => "p"} api={假api({ list: async () => ({ kind: "ok", value: [{ ...未读, read_at: 2 }] }) })} onOpen={() => {}} />
    ))
    await 等(() => host.textContent!.includes("没有新提醒"))
    expect(host.querySelector("[data-slot='case-alert-entry']")).not.toBeNull()
  })

  test("点入口回调 onOpen", async () => {
    let 次数 = 0
    const host = mount(() => <CaseAlertEntry projectId={() => "p"} api={假api()} onOpen={() => (次数 += 1)} />)
    await 等(() => host.querySelector("[data-slot='case-alert-count']") !== null)
    host.querySelector<HTMLElement>("[data-slot='case-alert-entry']")!.click()
    expect(次数).toBe(1)
  })

  test("没有项目 ⇒ 一句「未选择项目」，一次请求都不发", () => {
    let 发过 = 0
    const host = mount(() => (
      <CaseAlertEntry projectId={() => undefined} api={假api({ list: async () => { 发过 += 1; return { kind: "ok", value: [] } } })} onOpen={() => {}} />
    ))
    expect(host.textContent).toContain("未选择项目")
    expect(发过).toBe(0)
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 7: 实现前端 API 与入口**

`case-alerts-api.ts`（完整）：

```ts
import { PREFIX, 默认发送, 请求, type CaseFetch, type 结果 } from "./entity-api"

export interface 提醒行 {
  readonly id: string
  readonly rule_id: string
  readonly 标题: string
  readonly 内容: string
  readonly 事实: unknown
  readonly created_at: number
  readonly read_at: number | null
  readonly handled_at: number | null
}

export interface CaseAlertsApi {
  readonly list: (projectId: string) => Promise<结果<提醒行[]>>
  readonly refresh: (projectId: string) => Promise<结果<{ 新建: number; 提醒: 提醒行[] }>>
  readonly markRead: (projectId: string, ids: readonly string[]) => Promise<结果<{ 已读: number }>>
}

const 体 = (init: RequestInit) => ({ ...init, headers: { "content-type": "application/json", ...(init.headers ?? {}) } })

export function createCaseAlertsApi(send: CaseFetch = 默认发送): CaseAlertsApi {
  return {
    list: async (projectId) => {
      const 回 = await 请求<{ 提醒: 提醒行[] }>(send, `${PREFIX}/alerts?projectId=${encodeURIComponent(projectId)}`)
      return 回.kind === "error" ? 回 : { kind: "ok", value: 回.value.提醒 }
    },
    refresh: async (projectId) =>
      请求<{ 新建: number; 提醒: 提醒行[] }>(
        send,
        `${PREFIX}/alerts/refresh`,
        体({ method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify({ projectId }) }),
      ),
    markRead: async (projectId, ids) =>
      请求<{ 已读: number }>(
        send,
        `${PREFIX}/alerts/read`,
        体({ method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify({ projectId, ids }) }),
      ),
  }
}

/** 中栏提醒清单的伪路径。`all` 是这一版唯一的范围（按项目）。 */
export function 提醒路径(projectId: string): string {
  return `case://${encodeURIComponent(projectId)}/alerts/all`
}
```

`case-alert-entry.tsx`（完整）：

```tsx
import { createEffect, createMemo, createSignal, Show, type JSX } from "solid-js"
import { createCaseAlertsApi, type CaseAlertsApi, type 提醒行 } from "./case-alerts-api"

export interface CaseAlertEntryProps {
  readonly projectId: () => string | undefined
  readonly api?: CaseAlertsApi
  readonly onOpen: () => void
}

/**
 * 右栏会话顶部的「新提醒 N 条」（design §7.3）。
 *
 * **只读、不比对**：比对是写操作（`POST /alerts/refresh`），挂在用户看得见的「重新比对」按钮上
 * （中栏清单页）；右栏每次挂载都扫一遍全项目会把打开右栏变成一次重活。
 */
export function CaseAlertEntry(props: CaseAlertEntryProps): JSX.Element {
  // 默认客户端只在第一次用到时建（模块级常量会让测试替身与生产实例混住，同 `#002-02` 取向）。
  let 默认api: CaseAlertsApi | undefined
  const api = () => props.api ?? (默认api ??= createCaseAlertsApi())
  const [提醒, set提醒] = createSignal<readonly 提醒行[]>([])
  const [错误, set错误] = createSignal(false)

  createEffect(() => {
    const 项目 = props.projectId()
    const 客户端 = api()
    set提醒([])
    set错误(false)
    if (项目 === undefined) return
    let 作废 = false
    void 客户端.list(项目).then((结果) => {
      if (作废) return
      if (结果.kind === "error") {
        set错误(true)
        return
      }
      set提醒(结果.value)
    })
    return () => {
      作废 = true
    }
  })

  const 未读数 = createMemo(() => 提醒().filter((条) => 条.read_at === null).length)

  return (
    <Show when={props.projectId() !== undefined} fallback={<span data-slot="case-alert-entry" class="px-3 py-1 text-[12px] text-v2-text-text-muted">未选择项目</span>}>
      <button
        type="button"
        data-slot="case-alert-entry"
        data-unread={未读数()}
        class="flex w-full shrink-0 cursor-pointer items-center gap-1 border-b border-v2-border-border-muted px-3 py-1 text-start text-[12px] text-v2-text-text-muted hover:bg-v2-overlay-simple-overlay-hover"
        onClick={() => props.onOpen()}
      >
        <Show when={未读数() > 0} fallback={<span>没有新提醒</span>}>
          <span data-slot="case-alert-count" class="font-semibold text-v2-text-text-base">新提醒 {未读数()} 条</span>
        </Show>
        <Show when={错误()}>
          <span data-slot="case-alert-error" class="text-v2-state-fg-danger">读取失败</span>
        </Show>
      </button>
    </Show>
  )
}
```

> 注意 `let 默认api` 必须写在 `api()` 箭头函数**之前**：`let` 虽是提升的，但 TDZ 里第一次调用就是 `ReferenceError`。

`case-alert-entry-connected.tsx`（完整）：

```tsx
import type { JSX } from "solid-js"
import { useCenterTabs } from "@/center/tab-context"
import { currentProject } from "@/project/current-project"
import { CaseAlertEntry } from "./case-alert-entry"
import { 提醒路径 } from "./case-alerts-api"

/** 生产接线：右栏在 `CenterTabsProvider` 之内（`layout-new.tsx` 的 `right` 访问器），故能开中栏 tab。 */
export function CaseAlertEntryConnected(): JSX.Element {
  const center = useCenterTabs()
  return (
    <CaseAlertEntry
      projectId={() => currentProject()?.id}
      onOpen={() => {
        const 项目 = currentProject()?.id
        if (!项目) return
        center.open(
          { module: center.module() ?? "project", title: "提醒", path: 提醒路径(项目) },
          center.activePane(),
        )
      }}
    />
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: 实现中栏清单**

`case-views/alerts.tsx`（完整）：

```tsx
import { createEffect, createSignal, For, Show, type JSX } from "solid-js"
import type { CaseDetailApi } from "../case-detail-api"
import type { CaseAlertsApi, 提醒行 } from "../../project/case-alerts-api"
import { 时间文本 } from "./format"

export interface AlertsProps {
  readonly projectId: string
  /** 未用，但与本目录其它视图同一签名（`CaseView` 统一传）；不要为了「用上」去取无关数据。 */
  readonly api: CaseDetailApi
  readonly 提醒api: CaseAlertsApi
}

/**
 * 提醒清单（design §7.3）。**提醒只陈述数据事实**：这一页不出现「可疑」「建议立案」之类的结论。
 *
 * 「重新比对」是这一版唯一的触发点：常驻定时比对需要调度器，不在计划 7 的范围
 * （见文末缺口表）。按了才扫、扫完幂等落库（后端 `生成提醒`）。
 */
export function Alerts(props: AlertsProps): JSX.Element {
  const [行们, set行们] = createSignal<readonly 提醒行[]>([])
  const [错误, set错误] = createSignal<string | undefined>()
  const [新增话, set新增话] = createSignal<string | undefined>()
  const [载入, set载入] = createSignal(false)

  const 拉 = () => {
    set载入(true)
    let 作废 = false
    void props.提醒api.list(props.projectId).then((回) => {
      if (作废) return
      set载入(false)
      if (回.kind === "error") {
        set错误(回.message)
        return
      }
      set错误(undefined)
      set行们(回.value)
    })
    return () => {
      作废 = true
    }
  }

  createEffect(拉)

  const 重新比对 = () => {
    set载入(true)
    void props.提醒api.refresh(props.projectId).then((回) => {
      set载入(false)
      if (回.kind === "error") {
        set错误(回.message)
        return
      }
      set错误(undefined)
      set行们(回.value.提醒)
      set新增话(`新建 ${回.value.新建} 条`)
    })
  }

  const 标记 = (ids: readonly string[]) => {
    void props.提醒api.markRead(props.projectId, ids).then((回) => {
      if (回.kind === "error") {
        set错误(回.message)
        return
      }
      const 已 = new Set(ids)
      set行们((上一批) =>
        上一批.map((条) => (已.has(条.id) && 条.read_at === null ? { ...条, read_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) } : 条)),
      )
    })
  }

  return (
    <div data-component="alerts" class="flex min-h-0 w-full flex-1 flex-col overflow-y-auto px-3 py-2">
      <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <h1 class="text-[16px] font-semibold text-v2-text-text-base">提醒</h1>
        <button type="button" data-slot="alerts-refresh" onClick={重新比对} class="h-7 rounded-[4px] bg-v2-background-bg-layer-01 px-2 text-[13px] text-v2-text-text-base hover:bg-v2-overlay-simple-overlay-hover">
          重新比对
        </button>
        <button type="button" data-slot="alerts-read-all" onClick={() => 标记(行们().filter((条) => 条.read_at === null).map((条) => 条.id))} class="h-7 rounded-[4px] px-2 text-[13px] text-v2-text-text-muted hover:bg-v2-overlay-simple-overlay-hover">
          全部标为已读
        </button>
        <Show when={新增话()}>{(话) => <span data-slot="alerts-new-count" class="text-[12px] text-v2-text-text-muted">{话()}</span>}</Show>
      </div>

      <Show when={错误()}>{(话) => <p data-slot="alerts-error" role="alert" class="text-[13px] text-v2-state-fg-danger">{话()}</p>}</Show>
      <Show when={载入() && 行们().length === 0}>
        <p class="text-[13px] text-v2-text-text-muted">正在读取提醒…</p>
      </Show>
      <Show when={!载入() && 行们().length === 0 && 错误() === undefined}>
        <p data-slot="alerts-empty" class="text-[13px] text-v2-text-text-muted">没有提醒</p>
      </Show>

      <For each={行们()}>
        {(条) => (
          <div
            data-slot="alert-row"
            data-alert-id={条.id}
            data-read={条.read_at === null ? "false" : "true"}
            class="flex items-start gap-2 border-b border-v2-border-border-muted py-1 text-[13px]"
          >
            <Show when={条.read_at === null}>
              <span data-slot="alert-unread" class="mt-1 h-1.5 w-1.5 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-[var(--v2-state-fg-warning)]" />
            </Show>
            <div class="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <div class="text-v2-text-text-base">{条.标题}</div>
              <div class="text-v2-text-text-muted">{条.内容}</div>
              <div class="text-[12px] text-v2-text-text-muted">{时间文本(条.created_at)} · {条.rule_id}</div>
            </div>
            <Show when={条.read_at === null}>
              <button type="button" data-slot="alert-read" onClick={() => 标记([条.id])} class="shrink-0 rounded-[4px] px-1 text-[12px] text-v2-text-text-muted hover:bg-v2-overlay-simple-overlay-hover">
                已读
              </button>
            </Show>
          </div>
        )}
      </For>
    </div>
  )
}
```

- Produces:
```ts
// case-artifact-citations.ts
export interface 引用标记 {
  readonly fact_type: "entity" | "relation" | "event"
  readonly fact_id: string
  readonly 原文: string
  readonly 位置: number
}
export interface 未认标记 {
  readonly 原文: string
  readonly 原因: string
}
/** 纯函数：只判语法，不查库（查库那一半在 `登记产物引用` 里）。 */
export function 解析标记(content: string): { 引用们: readonly 引用标记[]; 未认: readonly 未认标记[] }

export interface 登记结果 {
  readonly 登记: number
  readonly 删除: number
  readonly 跳过: readonly 未认标记[]
  readonly 材料id: string | null
}
export async function 登记产物引用(
  db: Queryable,
  input: { readonly projectId: string; readonly path: string; readonly content: string },
): Promise<登记结果>

export interface 复核项 {
  readonly fact_type: string
  readonly fact_id: string
  readonly 值: string
  readonly 原文: string | null
  readonly 引用时间: number
  readonly 变更时间: number | null
  readonly 需复核: boolean
}
export interface 产物复核结果 {
  readonly 产物: {
    readonly id: string
    readonly path: string
    readonly file_name: string
    readonly kind: string
    readonly fingerprint: string
  } | null
  readonly 引用: readonly 复核项[]
  readonly 需复核: number
  readonly 缺口: string[]
}
export async function 产物复核(
  db: Queryable,
  input: { readonly projectId: string; readonly path: string },
): Promise<产物复核结果>

// HTTP
POST /openhive/case/artifacts/citations  {projectId, path, content} → 登记结果
GET  /openhive/case/artifacts/review?projectId&path                → 产物复核结果（200，永远不是 404）

// 前端 @/project/case-artifact-save
export async function 登记引用(
  projectId: string,
  path: string,
  content: string,
  send?: CaseFetch,
): Promise<结果<登记结果>>
export function createCaseArtifactSave(
  inner: SaveFileContent,
  send?: CaseFetch,
  projectId?: () => string | undefined,
): SaveFileContent

// 前端 @/center/case-detail-api（加一个字段）
readonly review: (projectId: string, path: string) => Promise<结果<产物复核结果>>

// 前端 @/center/case-views/artifact-review
export interface ArtifactReviewProps {
  readonly projectId: string
  readonly path: string
  readonly api: CaseDetailApi
}
export function ArtifactReview(props: ArtifactReviewProps): JSX.Element
```

**三条口径（写进 `case-artifact-citations.ts` 文件头，实现时不要私自放宽）**

1. **产物只登记、不解析**（design §5.5 的硬边界）。材料行 `kind='产物'`、`parse_status='已跳过'`、`parse_detail={"原因":"产物只登记不解析"}`。给产物开解析入口＝让报告进事实层，下一轮被当证据引回来。
2. **引用行与材料引用同表**（`case_citation`）：`material_id` = 产物那一行，`fact_type` / `fact_id` = 被引用的事实。同表能分清两个方向——「事实出自材料」那些行的 `material_id` 是原始材料，永远不会等于某个产物行的 id。
3. **「请复核」是查询派生的，不是写入态**（裁定 6，不加列、不加迁移）：`case_fact_change.created_at > case_citation.created_at` ⇒ 这条引用的依据变了。重复保存同一份产物会把**本路径**的旧引用删掉重登记（引用时间 = 现在），于是「请复核」自动消掉——**保存这个动作本身就是复核**。

`openhive-case-artifact-citations.test.ts`：`beforeAll` 起库照既有夹具（`startProductionDb` + `migrate`），用 `建实体` / `建关系` / `建事件` 种三条事实。

```ts
const 正文 = [
  "# 分析报告",
  "",
  "张三（[[fact:entity:e-张]]）与李四之间有大额转账（[[fact:relation:r-张李]]）。",
  "[[fact:entity:e-张]] 出现第二次，算同一条引用。",
  "这一条指向不存在的事实：[[fact:entity:e-没有]]。",
  "这一条类型不对：[[fact:row:话单.xlsx#Sheet1#2]]。",
].join("\n")

test("解析标记：认得三种事实、去重、报未认", () => {
  const { 引用们, 未认 } = 解析标记(正文)
  expect(引用们.map((条) => `${条.fact_type}:${条.fact_id}`)).toEqual(["entity:e-张", "relation:r-张李"])
  expect(引用们[0]!.原文).toBe("[[fact:entity:e-张]]")
  expect(引用们[0]!.位置).toBe(正文.indexOf("[[fact:entity:e-张]]"))
  expect(未认.map((条) => 条.原文)).toEqual(["[[fact:row:话单.xlsx#Sheet1#2]]"])
  expect(未认[0]!.原因).toContain("只认 entity / relation / event")
})

test("没写标记的正文 ⇒ 一条都不认，也不建材料行", async () => {
  const 结果 = await 登记产物引用(库(), { projectId: 项目, path: "报告/空.md", content: "没有标记" })
  expect(结果).toMatchObject({ 登记: 0, 删除: 0, 材料id: null })
  expect(await 材料行数(库(), 项目, "报告/空.md")).toBe(0)
})

test("登记：产物行进材料库，存在的两条写引用，不存在的进跳过", async () => {
  const 结果 = await 登记产物引用(库(), { projectId: 项目, path: "报告/分析.md", content: 正文 })
  expect(结果.登记).toBe(2)
  expect(结果.跳过.map((条) => 条.原文)).toEqual([
    "[[fact:entity:e-没有]]",
    "[[fact:row:话单.xlsx#Sheet1#2]]",
  ])
  const 材料 = await 材料行(库(), 项目, "报告/分析.md")
  expect(材料.kind).toBe("产物")
  expect(材料.parse_status).toBe("已跳过")
  expect(结果.材料id).toBe(材料.id)
})

test("重复登记同一份内容：材料行不分裂、引用条数不变", async () => {
  const 第一次 = await 登记产物引用(库(), { projectId: 项目, path: "报告/分析.md", content: 正文 })
  const 第二次 = await 登记产物引用(库(), { projectId: 项目, path: "报告/分析.md", content: 正文 })
  expect(第二次.材料id).toBe(第一次.材料id)
  expect(await 材料行数(库(), 项目, "报告/分析.md")).toBe(1)
  expect(await 引用行数(库(), 项目, 第二次.材料id!)).toBe(2)
})

test("内容变了 ⇒ 新版本材料行，旧版本的引用被清掉（同一路径只有一份回链）", async () => {
  const 旧的 = await 登记产物引用(库(), { projectId: 项目, path: "报告/改.md", content: 正文 })
  const 换了 = 正文 + "\n补充：[[fact:event:ev-见面]]。"
  const 新的 = await 登记产物引用(库(), { projectId: 项目, path: "报告/改.md", content: 换了 })
  expect(新的.材料id).not.toBe(旧的.材料id)
  expect(新的.登记).toBe(3)
  expect(await 引用行数(库(), 项目, 旧的.材料id!)).toBe(0)
})

test("产物复核：刚登记完没有要复核的", async () => {
  const 结果 = await 产物复核(库(), { projectId: 项目, path: "报告/分析.md" })
  expect(结果.产物?.kind).toBe("产物")
  expect(结果.引用.length).toBe(2)
  expect(结果.需复核).toBe(0)
})

test("这条事实之后被改过 ⇒ 恰好那一条要复核", async () => {
  const 前 = await 产物复核(库(), { projectId: 项目, path: "报告/分析.md" })
  const 引用时间 = 前.引用.find((条) => 条.fact_id === "e-张")!.引用时间
  await 建变更(库(), { projectId: 项目, factType: "entity", factId: "e-张", createdAt: 引用时间 + 1 })
  const 后 = await 产物复核(库(), { projectId: 项目, path: "报告/分析.md" })
  expect(后.需复核).toBe(1)
  expect(后.引用.find((条) => 条.fact_id === "e-张")!.需复核).toBe(true)
  expect(后.引用.find((条) => 条.fact_id === "r-张李")!.需复核).toBe(false)
})

test("没登记过的路径 ⇒ 产物 null ＋ 缺口一句话，不是抛错", async () => {
  const 结果 = await 产物复核(库(), { projectId: 项目, path: "报告/没有.md" })
  expect(结果.产物).toBeNull()
  expect(结果.缺口.join()).toContain("还没有登记过引用")
})
```

测试文件私有（**不要**用生产的 `记变更`：它写的是真实 `nowSeconds()`，与本进程刚写下的引用时间同秒 ⇒ `>` 判不出来，用例会时红时绿）：

```ts
async function 建变更(db: Queryable, input: { projectId: string; factType: string; factId: string; createdAt: number }) {
  await db.execute(sql`
    insert into auth.case_fact_change
      (id, project_id, fact_type, fact_id, action, actor_id, actor_name, actor_police_no, created_at)
    values (${crypto.randomUUID()}, ${input.projectId}, ${input.factType}, ${input.factId},
            '确认', 'u-测试', '测试员', '000000', ${input.createdAt})
  `)
}
// 三个小函数照既有夹具的「显式列序 ＋ 换行」写法，不复用生产 SQL 文本：
//   材料行数(db, projectId, path): count(*) from auth.case_material where …
//   材料行(db, projectId, path):   select id, kind, parse_status … order by created_at desc, id desc limit 1
//   引用行数(db, projectId, materialId): count(*) from auth.case_citation where …
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现 DAO**

`case-artifact-citations.ts` 开头（含文件头三条口径）：

```ts
export * as OpenhiveCaseArtifactCitations from "./case-artifact-citations"

import { basename } from "node:path"
import { Hash } from "@opencode-ai/core/util/hash"
import { rowsOf } from "@opencode-ai/auth/migrate"
import { nowSeconds } from "@opencode-ai/auth/time"
import { sql } from "drizzle-orm"
import type { Queryable } from "./case-store"

/** locator 是 jsonb：可能是对象（驱动已解析），也可能是字符串（旧行 / 某些驱动）。 */
const 安全解析 = (文本: string): unknown => {
  try {
    return JSON.parse(文本)
  } catch {
    return undefined
  }
}
```

> `安全解析` 与 `case-material-facts.ts` 里那个同名私有函数是**两份**：那一个是文件私有的，不跨文件复用（`#002-06` 的反面——这里复制的是 5 行纯函数，抽成公共模块反而给两处互相牵连留了口子）。若复审认为该抽，照复审意见改。

核心：

```ts
const 允许类型 = ["entity", "relation", "event"] as const
type 允许类型 = (typeof 允许类型)[number]

const 标记式 = /\[\[fact:([^\]\n]*)\]\]/g

export function 解析标记(content: string): { 引用们: readonly 引用标记[]; 未认: readonly 未认标记[] } {
  const 引用们: 引用标记[] = []
  const 未认: 未认标记[] = []
  const 见过 = new Set<string>()
  for (const 匹配 of content.matchAll(标记式)) {
    const 原文 = 匹配[0]
    const 段 = (匹配[1] ?? "").trim().split(":")
    if (段.length !== 2) {
      未认.push({ 原文, 原因: "标记要写成 [[fact:<类型>:<id>]]" })
      continue
    }
    const [类型, id] = [段[0]!.trim(), 段[1]!.trim()]
    if (!(允许类型 as readonly string[]).includes(类型)) {
      未认.push({ 原文, 原因: `只认 entity / relation / event，不认「${类型}」` })
      continue
    }
    if (id === "" || /\s/.test(id)) {
      未认.push({ 原文, 原因: "id 不能为空、不能含空白" })
      continue
    }
    const 键 = `${类型}:${id}`
    if (见过.has(键)) continue
    见过.add(键)
    引用们.push({ fact_type: 类型 as 允许类型, fact_id: id, 原文, 位置: 匹配.index ?? 0 })
  }
  return { 引用们, 未认 }
}
```

```ts
const 表名 = { entity: "auth.case_entity", relation: "auth.case_relation", event: "auth.case_event" } as const

export async function 登记产物引用(
  db: Queryable,
  input: { readonly projectId: string; readonly path: string; readonly content: string },
): Promise<登记结果> {
  const { 引用们, 未认 } = 解析标记(input.content)
  const 指纹 = Hash.fast(input.content)

  // 同内容已经以**别的类型**入过库 ⇒ 不翻它的类型、也不改它的解析状态（那份可能正在被解析）。
  const 同指纹 = rowsOf(await db.execute(sql`
    select id, kind from auth.case_material
     where project_id = ${input.projectId} and fingerprint = ${指纹}
     limit 1
  `))[0]
  if (同指纹 !== undefined && String(同指纹.kind) !== "产物")
    return {
      登记: 0,
      删除: 0,
      材料id: null,
      跳过: [...未认, { 原文: "", 原因: `同样的内容已作为「${String(同指纹.kind)}」入库，不登记为产物` }],
    }

  // 先清掉**本路径**旧版本的引用：引用行的核对基准是「现在这份正文」，旧版本的回链留着只会
  // 让「请复核」永远亮着。旧正文在盘上（重登记只是重算），所以清掉不是丢证据。
  const 删除 = rowsOf(await db.execute(sql`
    delete from auth.case_citation
     where project_id = ${input.projectId}
       and material_id in (
         select id from auth.case_material
          where project_id = ${input.projectId} and kind = '产物' and path = ${input.path}
       )
    returning id
  `)).length

  if (引用们.length === 0) return { 登记: 0, 删除, 材料id: null, 跳过: 未认 }

  const 材料id = await 登记产物行(db, input, 指纹)
  const 跳过: 未认标记[] = [...未认]
  let 登记 = 0
  for (const 类型 of 允许类型) {
    const 这批 = 引用们.filter((条) => 条.fact_type === 类型)
    if (这批.length === 0) continue
    const 在 = new Set(
      rowsOf(await db.execute(sql`
        select id from ${sql.raw(表名[类型])}
         where project_id = ${input.projectId}
           and id in (${sql.join(这批.map((条) => sql`${条.fact_id}`), sql`, `)})
      `)).map((行) => String(行.id)),
    )
    for (const 条 of 这批) {
      if (!在.has(条.fact_id)) {
        跳过.push({ 原文: 条.原文, 原因: "这个项目里没有这条事实" })
        continue
      }
      登记 += rowsOf(await db.execute(sql`
        insert into auth.case_citation
          (id, project_id, fact_type, fact_id, material_id, locator, extract_method, created_at)
        values (
          ${"cit-" + Hash.fast(`${input.projectId}|${材料id}|${条.fact_type}|${条.fact_id}`)},
          ${input.projectId}, ${条.fact_type}, ${条.fact_id}, ${材料id},
          ${JSON.stringify({ 类型: "正文标记", 序: 条.位置, 原文: 条.原文 })}::text::jsonb,
          '人工', ${nowSeconds()}
        )
        on conflict (project_id, id) do nothing
        returning id
      `)).length
    }
  }
  return { 登记, 删除, 材料id, 跳过 }
}
```

> ⚠️ `sql.raw(表名[类型])` 是**白名单查表**，不是拼接用户输入——`类型` 只可能是那三个字面量（`解析标记` 已挡过）。把这一句写进注释，否则复审会按注入报。

`登记产物行`（文件私有，单条语句、按 `(project_id, fingerprint)` 幂等——与 `createMaterial` 同键，但**多了「只在产物行上更新」这一道**，所以不能直接复用它）：

```ts
async function 登记产物行(
  db: Queryable,
  input: { readonly projectId: string; readonly path: string },
  指纹: string,
): Promise<string> {
  const 行们 = rowsOf(await db.execute(sql`
    insert into auth.case_material
      (id, project_id, file_name, kind, fingerprint, path, parse_status, parse_detail, created_at)
    values (
      ${"mat-" + Hash.fast(`${input.projectId}|${input.path}|${指纹}`)},
      ${input.projectId}, ${basename(input.path)}, '产物', ${指纹}, ${input.path},
      '已跳过', ${JSON.stringify({ 原因: "产物只登记不解析" })}::text::jsonb, ${nowSeconds()}
    )
    on conflict (project_id, fingerprint) do update
       set path = excluded.path, file_name = excluded.file_name
     where auth.case_material.kind = '产物'
    returning id
  `))
  const id = 行们[0]?.id
  if (typeof id !== "string") throw new Error(`登记产物没有拿到材料 id：${JSON.stringify(行们[0] ?? null)}`)
  return id
}
```

> `do update … where auth.case_material.kind = '产物'`：`fingerprint` 撞上一条**非产物**材料时，这条语句一行不返回（`WHERE` 挡住了更新），于是抛错、上层把这次登记记成失败。**这是有意 fail-closed**——比静默把别人的材料改成「已跳过」好。PG 的 `ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE` 支持 `WHERE`，`RETURNING` 只回真正更新过的行。

`产物复核`：

```ts
export async function 产物复核(
  db: Queryable,
  input: { readonly projectId: string; readonly path: string },
): Promise<产物复核结果> {
  const 材料 = rowsOf(await db.execute(sql`
    select id, path, file_name, kind, fingerprint
      from auth.case_material
     where project_id = ${input.projectId} and path = ${input.path}
     order by created_at desc, id desc
     limit 1
  `))[0]
  if (材料 === undefined)
    return {
      产物: null,
      引用: [],
      需复核: 0,
      缺口: ["这个路径下还没有登记过引用：保存一次带 [[fact:…]] 标记的报告就会登记"],
    }

  const 行们 = rowsOf(await db.execute(sql`
    with 变更 as (
      select fact_type, fact_id, max(created_at) as 变更时间
        from auth.case_fact_change
       where project_id = ${input.projectId}
       group by fact_type, fact_id
    )
    select c.id, c.fact_type, c.fact_id, c.locator, c.created_at,
           变更.变更时间,
           coalesce(实体.canonical_value,
                    (select 起.canonical_value || ' → ' || 终.canonical_value || ' · ' || 关系.type
                       from auth.case_relation 关系
                       join auth.case_entity 起 on 起.project_id = 关系.project_id and 起.id = 关系.from_id
                       join auth.case_entity 终 on 终.project_id = 关系.project_id and 终.id = 关系.to_id
                      where 关系.project_id = c.project_id and 关系.id = c.fact_id),
                    事件.summary,
                    c.fact_id) as 值
      from auth.case_citation c
      left join 变更 on 变更.fact_type = c.fact_type and 变更.fact_id = c.fact_id
      left join auth.case_entity 实体
        on 实体.project_id = c.project_id and c.fact_type = 'entity' and 实体.id = c.fact_id
      left join auth.case_event 事件
        on 事件.project_id = c.project_id and c.fact_type = 'event' and 事件.id = c.fact_id
     where c.project_id = ${input.projectId} and c.material_id = ${String(材料.id)}
     order by c.created_at desc, c.id asc
  `))

  const 引用 = 行们.map((行) => {
    const 引用时间 = Number(行.created_at)
    const 变更时间 = 行.变更时间 === null || 行.变更时间 === undefined ? null : Number(行.变更时间)
    return {
      fact_type: String(行.fact_type),
      fact_id: String(行.fact_id),
      值: String(行.值),
      原文: 取原文(行.locator),
      引用时间,
      变更时间,
      // 严格大于：同秒的那条变更不算（它可能是这次保存前后脚写下的，见文件头第 3 条）。
      需复核: 变更时间 !== null && 变更时间 > 引用时间,
    }
  })

  const 缺口: string[] = []
  if (String(材料.kind) !== "产物") 缺口.push(`这个路径最新的一条材料是「${String(材料.kind)}」，不是产物`)
  if (引用.length === 0) 缺口.push("这份产物没有引用任何事实")

  return {
    产物: {
      id: String(材料.id),
      path: String(材料.path),
      file_name: String(材料.file_name),
      kind: String(材料.kind),
      fingerprint: String(材料.fingerprint),
    },
    引用,
    需复核: 引用.filter((条) => 条.需复核).length,
    缺口,
  }
}

/** locator 里我们写的 `原文`；不是我们写的形状就给 null（不猜）。 */
function 取原文(locator: unknown): string | null {
  const 对象 = typeof locator === "string" ? 安全解析(locator) : locator
  if (typeof 对象 !== "object" || 对象 === null) return null
  const 原文 = (对象 as Record<string, unknown>)["原文"]
  return typeof 原文 === "string" ? 原文 : null
}
```

`openhive-case-ui-routes.test.ts` 末尾：

```ts
test("产物引用：登记 → 复核；没有标记的正文不建产物行", async () => {
```

```ts
  artifactReview: `${PREFIX}/artifacts/review`,
```

`routes` 里注册两条：

```ts

    yield* router.add("GET", PATH.artifactReview, (request) => handleArtifactReview(request, deps))
```

```ts
function handleArtifactCitations(request: HttpServerRequest.HttpServerRequest, deps: Deps) {
  return Effect.gen(function* () {
    const body = yield* 读体(request)
    const projectId = typeof body?.projectId === "string" ? body.projectId : null
    const 门 = yield* 过门(request, deps, projectId)
    if (是响应(门)) return 门
    const path = body?.path
    const content = body?.content
    if (typeof path !== "string" || path === "") return fail(400, "path 不能为空", "bad_request")
    if (typeof content !== "string") return fail(400, "content 必须是字符串", "bad_request")
    return ok(yield* Effect.promise(() => 登记产物引用(deps.pg(), { projectId: projectId!, path, content })))
  })
}

function handleArtifactReview(request: HttpServerRequest.HttpServerRequest, deps: Deps) {
  return Effect.gen(function* () {
    const 参数 = new URL(request.url, "http://localhost").searchParams
    const projectId = 参数.get("projectId")
    const 门 = yield* 过门(request, deps, projectId)
    if (是响应(门)) return 门
    const path = 参数.get("path")
    if (typeof path !== "string" || path === "") return fail(400, "path 不能为空", "bad_request")
    return ok(yield* Effect.promise(() => 产物复核(deps.pg(), { projectId: projectId!, path })))
  })
}
```
